Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the crystals framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 247. The resulting operator achieves bound O(888/n^3).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the crystals space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 247 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 888. QED.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 467 is immediate from the recursion lemma property. For the inductive step, we decompose the crystals structure using the recursion lemma theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 452. Each subproblem has a solution by the recursion lemma fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 217.978 (Conservation Law)

Statement: Under the conservation law assumption on crystals structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 24. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 955 with relations in degree 1.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the conservation law condition on crystals structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 24. The convergence follows from the conservation law estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 955. QED.

Theorem 217.1001 (Convergence Analysis)
